14	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
15	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
16	| 1 | `load_run_configs` | train.config | config path, seeds → list[RunConfig] | `tomllib` into frozen dataclasses, method defaults filled, name `<stem>-s<seed>` | TRAIN-01 |
17	| 2 | `check_plan` | train.check | list[RunConfig] → MethodConfig per method | each distinct team method with `check_settings` applied | TRAIN-06 |
18	| 3 | `train_checks` | train.check | MethodConfig per method, seed → Learner per method | make the method's check world and learner, step it for the fixed check budget | TRAIN-06 |
19	| 4 | `CheckDiscrete` | worlds.checks | overrides → CheckWorld | 6-state corridor, exits left of 0 (0.8) and right of 5 (1.0), γ = 0.9 (AD-09) | TRAIN-06 |

21	| 6 | `score_checks` | train.check | Learner per method, MethodConfig per method → list[CheckResult] | share of `states()` where `policy().act` matches `best_actions()` | TRAIN-06 |

24	```python
25	def run_check(config_path: Path, seed: int = 0) -> list[CheckResult]:
26	    configs = load_run_configs(config_path, [seed])
27	    plan = check_plan(configs)
28	    learners = train_checks(plan, seed)
29	    return score_checks(learners, plan)

57	## PL-03: Training batch
58	**Entry:** `src/rllab/train/pipelines/pl03_batch.py::run_batch` · **Trigger:** `rllab batch <config.toml> --seeds 1-5` (and `rllab train`, one seed)
59	**Covers:** TRAIN-05, TRAIN-06, QR-02
60	
61	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
62	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
63	| 1 | `load_run_configs` | train.config | config path, seeds → list[RunConfig] | PL-01 as built | TRAIN-01 |
64	| 2 | `check_plan` | train.check | list[RunConfig] → MethodConfig per method | PL-01 as built | TRAIN-06 |
65	| 3 | `train_checks` | train.check | MethodConfig per method, seed → Learner per method | PL-01 as built | TRAIN-06 |
66	| 4 | `score_checks` | train.check | Learner per method, MethodConfig per method → list[CheckResult] | PL-01 as built | TRAIN-06 |
67	| 5 | `require_pass` | train.check | list[CheckResult] → list[CheckResult] | raise `CheckFailedError` on the first failure | TRAIN-06 |
68	| 6 | `run_parallel` | train.batch | list[RunConfig], root, workers → list[RunRecord] | `spawn` process pool, one `run_train` (PL-02 as built) per config, one torch thread each (AD-05) | TRAIN-05, QR-02 |
69	
70	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
71	```python
72	def run_batch(config_path: Path, seeds: Sequence[int], root: Path, *, workers: int | None = None, check_seed: int = 0) -> list[RunRecord]:
73	    configs = load_run_configs(config_path, seeds)
74	    plan = check_plan(configs)
75	    learners = train_checks(plan, check_seed)
76	    results = score_checks(learners, plan)
77	    require_pass(results)
78	    return run_parallel(configs, root, workers)
79	```

138	## PL-08: Replay video
139	**Entry:** `src/rllab/cli/pipelines/pl08_video.py::run_video` · **Trigger:** `rllab video <world> <variant> <side> --seed N --out FILE [--sight]`
140	**Covers:** REPLAY-07, REPLAY-02, QR-06
141	
142	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
143	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
144	| 1 | `open_sources` | replay.sources | world, variant, side spec → Checkpoint or baseline per team | as `open_sides`, one side | REPLAY-07 |
145	| 2 | `make` | worlds.make | world, variant → World | PL-02 as built | WORLD-06 |
146	| 3 | `policies_for` | replay.sources | Checkpoint or baseline per team, WorldSpec → Policy per team | `methods.load_policy` or `baselines` per team; a team left out takes the first checkpoint's | REPLAY-01, REPLAY-06 |
147	| 4 | `play_episode` | core.episode | World, Policy per team, seed → Episode | reset, act per team, step until done or `max_steps`, frames kept | REPLAY-02, WORLD-02 |
148	| 5 | `to_trace` | replay.tracing | Episode, WorldSpec, label → Trace | copy frames, set label and step-limit flag | REPLAY-02 |
149	| 6 | `save_video` | replay.video | Trace, out → Output | draw each frame off-screen, imageio-ffmpeg H.264 at 30 fps | REPLAY-07, QR-06 |
150	
151	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
152	```python
153	def run_video(world: str, variant: str, side: str, seed: int, out: Path, *, overlay: bool = False) -> Output:
154	    sources = open_sources(world, variant, side)
155	    w = make(world, variant)
156	    policies = policies_for(sources, w.spec)
157	    episode = play_episode(w, policies, seed, trace=True)
158	    trace = to_trace(episode, w.spec, side)
159	    return save_video(trace, out, overlay_sight=overlay)
160	```

163	### core.ActionSpace: type
164	`ActionSpace = Discrete(n: int) | Box(dims: int, low: float, high: float)`, both frozen dataclasses
165	- **Invariants:** `Discrete` actions are ints in `[0, n)`; `Box` actions are `dims` floats in `[low, high]`
166	- **Boundary:** worlds, baselines, methods, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.ActionSpace/ · **Cases:** snake `Discrete(3)`; fish `Box(2, -1.0, 1.0)`
167	- **Used by:** worlds, baselines, methods, refcheck
168	
169	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:15 -->
170	### core.TeamSpec: type
171	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TeamSpec: name: str; agents: tuple[int, ...]; score_name: str; direction: Direction`
172	- **Invariants:** `agents` are indices into the world's agent axis; teams of one world are disjoint and cover every agent
173	- **Boundary:** worlds, train, evaluate, charts · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.TeamSpec/ · **Cases:** snake's one team; predator-prey's prey (6) and predator (2) teams
174	- **Used by:** every component reading scores
175	
176	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:21 -->
177	### core.WorldSpec: type
178	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class WorldSpec: world: str; variant: str; agents: tuple[str, ...]; teams: tuple[TeamSpec, ...]; obs_len: int; action: ActionSpace; max_steps: int; measures: tuple[MeasureSpec, ...]; sight: float | None`
179	`MeasureSpec(name: str, team: str, direction: Direction)`
180	- **Invariants:** available without running an episode (WORLD-04); `sight` is the radius in world units for local-sight variants, else `None`; equality of two specs is field by field
181	- **Boundary:** worlds, methods, runlog, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.WorldSpec/ · **Cases:** snake default; fish local-sight (8 agents, sight set); predator-prey
182	- **Used by:** all components; PL-02, PL-08
183	
184	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:28 -->
185	### core.Step: type
186	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Step: obs: Array; rewards: Array; active: Array; done: bool; truncated: bool`
187	- **Invariants:** `obs` float32 `n_agents × obs_len`; `rewards` float32 `n_agents`; `active` bool `n_agents`, and an inactive agent's row is zeros with reward 0 (WORLD edge case); `truncated` is true only when `max_steps` ended the episode
188	- **Boundary:** worlds, methods, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Step/ · **Cases:** a snake mid-episode step; a predator-prey step with one prey caught
189	- **Used by:** core.play_episode, train, refcheck
190	
191	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:34 -->
192	### core.EpisodeResult: type
193	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class EpisodeResult: seed: int; length: int; scores: Mapping[str, float]; team_rewards: Mapping[str, float]; measures: Mapping[str, float]`
194	- **Invariants:** `scores` and `team_rewards` keyed by team name (WORLD-05, LOG-02); `measures` keyed by every `MeasureSpec.name`
195	- **Boundary:** train, runlog, evaluate, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.EpisodeResult/ · **Cases:** a snake episode with 12 food; a predator-prey episode with both team scores
196	- **Used by:** train, runlog, evaluate, refcheck

199	### core.DrawState: type
200	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class DrawState: width: float; height: float; objects: tuple[Drawable, ...]`
201	`Drawable(kind: str, x: float, y: float, size: float, team: str | None, agent: int | None)`
202	- **Invariants:** lists every object on screen at that step (WORLD-06); snake cells use grid coordinates with `size` 1; `agent` set for agents, so the viewer can draw `WorldSpec.sight` around them
203	- **Boundary:** worlds, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.DrawState/ · **Cases:** a snake board; an 8-fish tank with the ball
204	- **Used by:** replay

207	### core.Frame: type
208	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Frame: step: int; draw: DrawState; scores: Mapping[str, float]; rewards: Array`
209	- **Invariants:** `scores` is each team's running score after this step; `rewards` the latest per agent (REPLAY-05); frame 0 is the reset state with zero rewards
210	- **Boundary:** replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Frame/ · **Cases:** frame 0 and frame 1 of seed 7 snake
211	- **Used by:** replay

214	### core.Episode: type
215	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Episode: result: EpisodeResult; frames: tuple[Frame, ...] | None; hit_step_limit: bool`
216	- **Invariants:** `frames` is set only when traced, with `result.length + 1` entries
217	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Episode/ · **Cases:** an untraced snake episode; a traced 3-step check-world episode
218	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck; PL-08

220	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:59 -->
221	### core.World: type
222	```python
223	class World(Protocol):
224	    spec: WorldSpec
225	    def reset(self, seed: int) -> Step: ...
226	    def step(self, actions: Array) -> Step: ...
227	    def result(self) -> EpisodeResult: ...
228	    def draw(self) -> DrawState: ...
229	```
230	- **Invariants:** same seed → same start (WORLD-01); same seed and actions → same steps (WORLD-02); `actions` has one row per agent, rows of inactive agents ignored
231	- **Errors:** `step` raises `ActionError(agent: str, value: object)`, a `ValueError`, for an action outside `spec.action`, never clipping it; `step` after `done` raises `RuntimeError`
232	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.World/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 5-step snake episode from seed 42
233	- **Used by:** every component that plays episodes; PL-02, PL-08
234	
235	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:73 -->
236	### core.Policy: type
237	`class Policy(Protocol): def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array`
238	- **Invariants:** `obs` is `k × obs_len` for the `k` agents of one team; returns `k` actions inside the world's `ActionSpace`; deterministic for a given `obs` and `rng` state
239	- **Boundary:** baselines, methods, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Policy/ · **Cases:** a replay double of the snake rule policy over 5 observations
240	- **Used by:** core.play_episode, train, evaluate, replay, refcheck; PL-08

242	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:79 -->
243	### core.play_episode: The one episode loop
244	`def play_episode(world: World, policies: Mapping[str, Policy], seed: int, *, trace: bool = False) -> Episode`
245	- **Invariants:** `policies` keyed by team; the seed spawns the world's and each policy's generator (AD-05), so the same inputs give the same `Episode`; stops at `done` or `spec.max_steps`
246	- **Errors:** `KeyError` naming a team with no policy; `ActionError` propagates from `World.step`
247	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.play_episode/ · **Cases:** snake rule policy, seed 7, traced
248	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck; PL-02, PL-08

334	### methods.Learner: type
335	```python
336	class Learner(Protocol):
337	    def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array: ...   # exploring
338	    def observe(self, t: Transition) -> None: ...                       # updates on its own schedule
339	    def policy(self) -> Policy: ...                                      # frozen, deterministic copy
340	    def state_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]: ...
341	```
342	- **Invariants:** hand-written learning code only (CHECK-01); `policy()` is greedy (DQN) or the mean action (PPO) and does not change as the learner trains
343	- **Errors:** `observe` raises `NonFiniteError(value: float)` when a loss or parameter is NaN or infinite, before changing any parameter
344	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/methods.Learner/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a DQN learner over 10 snake transitions
345	- **Used by:** train; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03

376	`def run_check(config_path: Path, seed: int = 0) -> list[CheckResult]`
377	- **Invariants:** one result per distinct team method in the config, trained on its check world (`check-discrete` for `dqn`, `check-continuous` for `ppo`) with `check_settings`, for the fixed check budget; under a minute on the laptop (TRAIN-06, AD-09)

381	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:215 -->
382	### train.run_train: One training run
383	`def run_train(config: RunConfig, root: Path) -> RunRecord`
384	- **Invariants:** exactly `budget` world steps (TRAIN-01); checkpoints at 0%, 10%, … 100% (TRAIN-03); evaluation of every team on `EVAL_SEEDS` at every 5% (TRAIN-04); same config on the same machine → identical logs and checkpoints (TRAIN-02)
385	- **Errors:** `RunExistsError` before any work if the run folder exists; `NonFiniteError` ends the run as `failed` with step and value, keeping the last good checkpoint; interruption marks it `incomplete`
386	- **Boundary:** PL-03 · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.run_train/ · **Cases:** a fake writing the finished 3-checkpoint snake run folder
387	- **Used by:** train.run_batch; PL-02, PL-03

389	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:222 -->
390	### train.run_batch: Training batch
391	`def run_batch(config_path: Path, seeds: Sequence[int], root: Path, *, workers: int | None = None, check_seed: int = 0) -> list[RunRecord]`
392	- **Invariants:** loads one config per seed, runs the method check with `check_seed` before any run, then one process per config up to `workers` (default: the core count), each with one torch thread (TRAIN-05, TRAIN-06, AD-05)
393	- **Errors:** `CheckFailedError(result: CheckResult)` before any run starts; a failing run doesn't stop the others
394	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.run_batch/ · **Cases:** a fake returning 2 finished snake records
395	- **Used by:** cli; PL-03

400	- **Invariants:** `agreement` is the share of check-world states where the trained policy picks the best action; `passed` is `agreement >= 0.95` (TRAIN-06)
401	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.CheckResult/ · **Cases:** a passing DQN check; a failing PPO check at 0.80

492	### evaluate.Comparison: type
493	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Comparison: baseline: Literal["random", "rule"]; team: str; learned_mean: float; baseline_mean: float; difference: float; p_raw: float; p_holm: float; rejected: bool`
494	- **Invariants:** `difference` is signed so positive means better in the team's direction
495	- **Boundary:** refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.Comparison/ · **Cases:** a rejected snake-vs-random; a kept snake-vs-rule

498	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:330 -->
499	### evaluate.compare: Paired permutation test
500	`def compare(learned: Array, baseline: Array, direction: Direction, *, permutations: int = 10_000, rng: np.random.Generator) -> tuple[float, float]`
501	- **Invariants:** `learned` is runs × seeds, averaged over runs per seed; `baseline` has one score per seed, on the same seeds; swaps labels within each seed; returns (difference, one-sided p) as EVAL-04 states; built and checked with the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill
502	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if seed counts differ
503	- **Used by:** evaluate.final_evaluation

505	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:336 -->
506	### evaluate.Summary: type
507	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Summary: world: str; variant: str; team: str; runs: tuple[str, ...]; learned_mean: float; learned_range: tuple[float, float]; run_means: Mapping[str, float]; random_mean: float; rule_mean: float; comparisons: tuple[Comparison, ...]; verdict: Verdict; collapse: int | None`
508	`Verdict(beats_baselines: bool, failing_runs: tuple[str, ...], runs_needed: int)`
509	- **Invariants:** one per world, variant and team (EVAL-06, PREY-08); `comparisons` empty and `runs_needed > 0` with fewer than 5 runs; `collapse` is set when the team's in-training eval score stays at or below its random-baseline mean for the last 4+ consecutive eval points, and is the step of the first of them
510	- **Boundary:** charts, refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.Summary/ · **Cases:** a passing snake summary; one with a failing run; one with 3 runs
511	- **Used by:** refcheck, cli; PL-06
512	
513	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:343 -->
514	### evaluate.final_evaluation: Final evaluation
515	`def final_evaluation(runs: Sequence[Run], out: Path, *, workers: int | None = None) -> list[Summary]`
516	- **Invariants:** plays every final checkpoint and both baselines on `FINAL_SEEDS` (EVAL-03); Holm across the 2 comparisons per team; a pass needs both nulls rejected (EVAL-04) and every run's mean beating the rule mean (EVAL-05); in predator-prey each team plays against the rule-based opponent (PREY-08); within 10 minutes (QR-05)
517	- **Boundary:** refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.final_evaluation/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the passing snake summary
518	- **Used by:** refcheck, cli; PL-06

520	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:466 -->
521	### cli.main: The rllab command
522	`def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int`
523	- **Invariants:** subcommands `check`, `train`, `batch`, `watch`, `chart`, `evaluate`, `grid`, `refcheck`, `replay`, `video`, `notes new`, `notes check`, each dispatched to its pipeline entry (`train` is a one-seed `batch`); prints the entry's returned contract type; owns no pipeline step (AD-10); every error is printed as one line naming its cause, exit code 1
524	- **Used by:** the learner

56	## Review
57	Run by the reviewer agent over what it is given (base, package, tasks, paths): the interface review after Phase 0, a phase review per package at each checkpoint, and the milestone exit. `dt land` already checked each landing's `Writes:`, protected paths and gates; review judges what tools can't.
58	
59	**Interface** (after Phase 0): the code is the contract, no more and no less.
60	1. Types match `contract.md`: fields, types, invariants. Nothing a step's In → Out needs is missing; no helper is frozen.
61	2. Signatures match the step tables, in their owner modules. Each stub names the open task that owns its step; no logic beyond types and validation.
62	3. One sample per case the contract names, valid and realistically shaped, never carrying a TC's expected value. Replay doubles replay an interaction the Protocol allows; fakes serve their fixtures.
63	4. `__init__.py` exports exactly the contract; each `Mitigated by` in the plan's dependency graph exists; each split task's tester can write its tests from the frozen interface and its `Read:`.
64	
65	**Each phase**, per package, tests before code:
66	1. **Interfaces followed** (CR-09): no frozen type, signature or contract folder changed outside a Revise. Consumers use the declared types: no dict for a dataclass, no reaching into a provider's internals, no local redefinition. A stub is replaced only by the task it names; no cross-package import outside the contract; spine tests run on samples and doubles, not another step's code.
67	2. **Tests prove something:** each intent test asserts its TC case or acceptance bullet, loading expected values and tolerances from fixtures; none is assertion-free or weakened; Hypothesis runs the `ci` profile. Unit tests are specific, never computing the expected value with the code under test or testing only a mock. **Mutation check** (`.claude/agents/reviewer.md`): per acceptance bullet, the smallest change that should break it; a mutation no test catches is a finding.
68	3. **Correct to the spec:** each acceptance bullet truly met, not special-cased to the sample; the error paths the requirement names; seeding and determinism; numerical edge cases.
69	4. **Simple** (CR-11, CR-12, CR-42): no abstraction with one implementation outside the contract or an AD; no forwarding wrapper, one-method class, or class where a function does; no parameter, flag or config key every caller sets the same; no `try/except` that only re-raises or swallows, no checks the types already guarantee; no loop the library already provides. No near-copies within or across the phase's tasks: one concept is one function in its owner module (tests may repeat for readability). Each finding names the simpler form, with the same tests passing.
70	5. **Pipelines** (CR-10): entry functions read as their step tables (`dt layout`); step owners respected; spine baselines still selectable.
71	6. **The bar** (CR-07): diff this file, `pyproject.toml`, `scripts/` and `.claude/agents/` against the milestone's Base. Any loosening is Critical; new exceptions need an owner and expiry.
72	7. **Hidden and parallel risks:** where a real provider could act differently from its double (flagged for the system phase); fixed output paths, global state, test-order dependence.
73	8. **The rest:** readability, security, performance where a QR sets a number; reused reference or probe code has its Caveats handled and fits the contract; `dt lint` clean, pre-build sections replaced by `dt layout` pointers.
74	
75	**Milestone exit**, over the whole diff, adds: the Scope's Exit holds; every Covers ID has a test; `check.sh full --exit` finds no stub; system tests use real providers; the same sample through a real provider and its double agrees; deferred requirements untouched; pip-audit ran.
76	
77	**Severity:** Critical: a frozen interface changed, a weakened test, an intent test that misses a mutation changing its TC's expected output, a wrong answer, a loosened bar, a security hole, lost data; it stops the build. Important: a consumer bypassing a type, any other surviving mutation, an unneeded abstraction, a duplicate across tasks. Suggestion and Nit: local. Findings go under the checkpoint block in `tasks/plan.md` (`dt finding`), each with a fix task or a reason.

 - **Alternatives:** Split `refcheck` into its own package: a real boundary, but already met by the extra. Split the shared core from the worlds: every world needs the core, and the core has no other consumer.
 - **Consequences:** The Grow triggers stay reported by `dt` and are answered by this decision. A new trigger (a part that runs on its own, a heavy new stack) reopens it.
 
+### AD-12: Run and method settings types live in `core`
+**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-18 · **Scope:** system
+- **Context:** The M1 interface task found that `runlog.RunRecord`, `runlog.RunWriter` and `runlog.Checkpoint` hold a run's config and per-team method configs (LOG-01, AD-06), whose types `train` and `methods` owned. `runlog` may import only `core`, and import-linter counts `TYPE_CHECKING` imports too, so no version of those types could be written.
+- **Decision:** A type that a lower component stores but a higher one fills lives in `core`. `RunConfig`, `MethodConfig` and the `Settings` alias are `core` types. `methods` still declares each method's keys and defaults, and `train.load_run_configs` still builds and fills the configs; neither owns the types.
+- **Alternatives:** Move `RunRecord`, `Checkpoint` and `RunWriter` up into `train`: `methods`, `evaluate`, `replay` and `charts` read checkpoints and records, so they would all depend on `train`, and the batch's worker processes would import them. Store the config in `runlog` as an untyped mapping: loses the frozen type at every reader.
+- **Consequences:** `runlog` keeps `core` as its only dependency, and the import layers are unchanged. Every consumer imports `RunConfig` and `MethodConfig` from `core`.
+
 ## Risks and open questions
 - **Many-fish and predator-prey speed (QR-01, QR-02).** 8–10 agents, 1,000-step episodes and 21 × 20 evaluation episodes may not fit 60 minutes in Python. Mitigation: NumPy physics over all agents, evaluation in a worker process, and a timing check in the first fish build; QR-03's GPU route doesn't help a CPU-bound world loop.
 - **5 parallel runs on mixed cores (QR-02).** Apple Silicon's efficiency cores run slower; 5 runs may not all land on performance cores. Measured in the first snake batch.

3	# Written by dev-team2 build-setup. Change it through build-setup (Revise), not by hand:
4	# CONSTRAINTS.md names the rule each step enforces.
5	#
6	#   scripts/check.sh fast FILE...        per edit, instant               (PostToolUse: tester, implementer)
7	#   scripts/check.sh collect             tester's finish gate            (Stop: tester)
8	#   scripts/check.sh gate [--pkg ID]     implementer's finish gate       (Stop: implementer and solo; dt land)
9	#   scripts/check.sh full [--pkg ID]     checkpoints, CI                 (reviewer)
10	#   scripts/check.sh full --exit         the milestone exit: also no interface stub left
11	#   scripts/check.sh protected           print the protected-path globs  (path_guard.sh deny --protected)
12	#
13	# The base is refs/worktree/checks-base, one per worktree (dt claim sets it in a builder's worktree,
14	# dt land in the main checkout), else HEAD.
15	#
16	# tests/contracts/** in the protected list means the boundary contracts frozen at the base (CR-09);
17	# a contract folder new since the base belongs to the interface task freezing it, which may write it,
18	# and so does a frozen folder the task's .claude/.task_writes names (a re-freeze interface task).
19	#
20	# collect runs the intent tests changed since the base and fails unless they are red for the right
21	# reason (scripts/red_check.py): AssertionError or an interface stub's NotImplementedError, never
22	# an ImportError or AttributeError.
23	#
24	# --pkg ID runs the tests of package ID and of every package that depends on it (multi-package:
25	# deptry too); gate and full also run tests/contracts, whole, when it exists. Lint, types, size
26	# and boundaries always cover the whole repo; they take seconds.
27	# FILE paths are absolute or relative to the project root.
28	# Prints every failure (it does not stop at the first) and exits 1 if any step failed.
29	set -u
30	cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." || exit 1   # hooks may run from another directory
31	export NO_COLOR=1                   # plain output for the agent reading it
32	
33	# ---- Generated by build-setup. Do not edit by hand. ----------------------------------------
34	# The protected list (CONSTRAINTS.md, "Roles and protected paths"). One list serves as git
35	# pathspecs here and as path_guard.sh globs; in both, * also matches /.
36	PROTECTED=('tests/intent/**' 'tests/fixtures/**' 'tests/conftest.py' 'pyproject.toml' 'uv.lock' 'CONSTRAINTS.md' 'scripts/**' '.claude/agents/**' '.claude/settings.json' 'references/**' 'probes/**' 'tests/contracts/**')
37	SRC_DIRS=(src)            # single package: src · multi-package: packages/<id>/src ...
38	PACKAGES_DIR=""    # empty for a single package; multi-package: packages
39	TYPE_GATE="mypy"          # mypy | pyright: the type checker that gates (CR-23); full runs both
40	
41	# A package (or, in a single package, a component) and every package that depends on it,
42	# from the architecture's dependency rules.
43	pkg_scope() {
44	  case "$1" in
45	    core) echo "core worlds baselines methods train runlog charts evaluate replay refcheck cli" ;;
46	    worlds) echo "worlds baselines train charts evaluate replay refcheck cli" ;;
47	    baselines) echo "baselines train charts evaluate replay refcheck cli" ;;
48	    methods) echo "methods train charts evaluate replay refcheck cli" ;;
49	    train) echo "train refcheck cli" ;;
50	    runlog) echo "runlog methods train charts evaluate replay refcheck cli" ;;
51	    charts) echo "charts cli" ;;
52	    evaluate) echo "evaluate charts refcheck cli" ;;
53	    replay) echo "replay cli" ;;
54	    refcheck) echo "refcheck cli" ;;
55	    notes) echo "notes cli" ;;
56	    cli) echo "cli" ;;
57	    *) return 1 ;;
58	  esac
59	}
60	
61	# Non-Python parts (CONSTRAINTS.md names them): their own check commands, run in gate and full.
62	other_parts() {
63	  : # e.g. step npm --prefix web run check
64	}
65	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
66	
67	mode="${1:-gate}"
68	shift || true
69	pkg=""
70	exit_check=()
71	args=()
72	while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
73	  case "$1" in
74	    --pkg) pkg="${2:-}"; shift 2 || shift ;;
75	    --exit) exit_check=(--exit); shift ;;
76	    *) args+=("$1"); shift ;;
77	  esac
78	done
79	
80	if [ -n "$pkg" ] && ! pkg_scope "$pkg" >/dev/null; then
81	  echo "unknown package '$pkg' (see pkg_scope in scripts/check.sh)" >&2
82	  exit 64
83	fi
84	
85	rc=0
86	failed=()
87	
88	# A step that is not a Python tool (a script, git, another toolchain).
89	step() {
90	  echo "-> $*" >&2
91	  "$@" || { rc=1; failed+=("$*"); }
92	}
93	
94	# A tool from the project's locked environment, never whatever is first on PATH.
95	run() {
96	  echo "-> $*" >&2
97	  uv run --frozen --quiet "$@" || { rc=1; failed+=("$*"); }
98	}
99	
100	# A measured check that fails only at the milestone exit: it prints its findings, and the reviewer
101	# records them, but a mid-milestone run passes.
102	report() {
103	  echo "-> $* (reported; fails only with --exit)" >&2
104	  uv run --frozen --quiet "$@" || echo "(reported, not failing: $*)" >&2
105	}
106	
107	# pytest exits 5 when it collects nothing. That is fine for gate and full on a project with no
108	# tests yet; collect calls pytest through run() so an empty tests/intent fails.
109	run_tests() {
110	  echo "-> pytest $*" >&2
111	  uv run --frozen --quiet pytest "$@"
112	  local status=$?
113	  [ "$status" -eq 5 ] && { echo "(no tests collected)" >&2; return; }
114	  [ "$status" -eq 0 ] || { rc=1; failed+=("pytest $*"); }
115	}
116	
117	# The commit this task started from: refs/worktree/checks-base, set per worktree by `dt claim` (and
118	# by `dt land` in the main checkout). Without it, HEAD, which misses changes already committed.
119	BASE_REF="refs/worktree/checks-base"
120	base_commit() {
121	  git rev-parse -q --verify "$BASE_REF" 2>/dev/null || git rev-parse -q --verify HEAD 2>/dev/null
122	}
123	
124	# A boundary contract is frozen once its folder exists at the base (CR-09): each folder under
125	# tests/contracts/ at the base, and any file directly in it. A folder new since the base is the
126	# interface task freezing it, so that task may write it; the next task's base freezes it. A frozen
127	# folder the task's .claude/.task_writes names (from its Writes:, by dt claim) is a re-freeze
128	# interface task's, so it is open to that task; dt land checks the Writes: against the plan.
129	CONTRACTS_GLOB="tests/contracts/**"
130	claimed_contract() {
131	  local line
132	  [ -s .claude/.task_writes ] || return 1
133	  while IFS= read -r line; do
134	    line="${line%%[[:space:]]}"
135	    line="${line%/}"; line="${line%/\*\*}"
136	    [ "$line" = "$1" ] && return 0
137	  done <.claude/.task_writes
138	  return 1
139	}
140	frozen_contracts() {
141	  local base meta path
142	  base=$(base_commit) || return 0
143	  git ls-tree "$base" -- tests/contracts/ 2>/dev/null | while IFS=$'\t' read -r meta path; do
144	    case "$meta" in
145	      *" tree "*) claimed_contract "$path" || echo "$path/**" ;;
146	      *) echo "$path" ;;
147	    esac
148	  done
149	}
150	
151	# The protected list as enforced: PROTECTED, with tests/contracts/** narrowed to the frozen contracts.
152	protected_globs() {
153	  local g
154	  for g in "${PROTECTED[@]}"; do
155	    if [ "$g" = "$CONTRACTS_GLOB" ]; then frozen_contracts; else echo "$g"; fi
156	  done
157	}
158	
159	# Fail if anything matching the given pathspecs changed since the base, untracked files included.
160	unchanged_since_base() {
161	  local label="$1" base new
162	  shift
163	  echo "-> $label unchanged since $(git rev-parse -q --verify "$BASE_REF" >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "$BASE_REF" || echo HEAD)" >&2
164	  if ! base=$(base_commit); then
165	    echo "no commit to compare against: commit the tester's work first" >&2
166	    rc=1; failed+=("$label: no commit"); return
167	  fi
168	  git --no-pager diff --exit-code --stat "$base" -- "$@" >&2 || { rc=1; failed+=("$label changed"); }
169	  new=$(git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- "$@")
170	  if [ -n "$new" ]; then
171	    printf 'new untracked files in %s:\n%s\n' "$label" "$new" >&2
172	    rc=1; failed+=("$label changed")
173	  fi
174	}
175	
176	# The tester may change tests/intent only. Docs and plans are the plugin's business, not this check's.
177	only_intent_changed() {
178	  local specs=(tests "${SRC_DIRS[@]}" "${PROTECTED[@]}")
179	  [ -n "$PACKAGES_DIR" ] && specs+=("$PACKAGES_DIR")
180	  unchanged_since_base "files outside tests/intent" "${specs[@]}" ':(exclude)tests/intent'
181	}
182	
183	# The intent test files the tester changed since the base, committed or not.
184	changed_intent_tests() {
185	  local base
186	  base=$(base_commit) || return 0
187	  { git diff --relative --name-only --diff-filter=d "$base" -- 'tests/intent/*.py'
188	    git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- 'tests/intent/*.py'; } | sort -u |
189	    while IFS= read -r f; do case "${f##*/}" in test_*|*_test.py) echo "$f" ;; esac; done
190	}
191	
192	# Test directories for this run: all of them, or the --pkg scope; tests/contracts always whole.
193	test_dirs() {
194	  local scope="" d p
195	  [ -n "$pkg" ] && scope=$(pkg_scope "$pkg")
196	  for d in tests/intent tests/unit; do
197	    if [ -z "$scope" ]; then
198	      [ -d "$d" ] && echo "$d"
199	    else
200	      for p in $scope; do [ -d "$d/$p" ] && echo "$d/$p"; done
201	    fi
202	  done
203	  [ -d tests/contracts ] && echo tests/contracts
204	}
205	
206	# deptry checks what each package imports against what it declares.
207	dependencies() {
208	  local p scope
209	  if [ -z "$PACKAGES_DIR" ]; then
210	    run deptry . --no-ansi
211	    return
212	  fi
213	  if [ -n "$pkg" ]; then
214	    scope=$(pkg_scope "$pkg")
215	  else
216	    scope=$(cd "$PACKAGES_DIR" && for d in */pyproject.toml; do [ -f "$d" ] && dirname "$d"; done)
217	  fi
218	  for p in $scope; do
219	    echo "-> deptry $PACKAGES_DIR/$p" >&2
220	    (cd "$PACKAGES_DIR/$p" && uv run --frozen --quiet deptry . --no-ansi) || { rc=1; failed+=("deptry $PACKAGES_DIR/$p"); }
221	  done
222	}
223	
224	static_checks() {
225	  run ruff check --ignore-noqa .   # exceptions go in per-file-ignores, which is protected (CR-01)
226	  run ruff format --check .
227	  run "$TYPE_GATE"
228	  run python scripts/check_size.py "${SRC_DIRS[@]}"
229	  dependencies
230	  run lint-imports --no-logo
231	}
232	
233	case "$mode" in
234	  fast)
235	    # Only Python files, only the files just edited. Fixes what it can in place.
236	    files=()
237	    for f in ${args[@]+"${args[@]}"}; do [[ $f == *.py ]] && files+=("$f"); done
238	    [ ${#files[@]} -eq 0 ] && exit 0
239	    run ruff check --fix --quiet "${files[@]}"
240	    run ruff format --quiet "${files[@]}"
241	    run typos "${files[@]}"
242	    run python scripts/check_size.py "${files[@]}"
243	    ;;
244	  collect)
245	    if [ ! -d tests/intent ]; then
246	      echo "tests/intent does not exist: write the intent tests there" >&2
247	      exit 1
248	    fi
249	    run ruff check --ignore-noqa tests/intent
250	    run ruff format --check tests/intent
251	    run typos tests/intent
252	    run pytest tests/intent --collect-only -q --no-cov
253	    changed=()
254	    while IFS= read -r f; do changed+=("$f"); done < <(changed_intent_tests)
255	    run python scripts/red_check.py ${changed[@]+"${changed[@]}"}
256	    step bash scripts/floor_guard.sh
257	    only_intent_changed
258	    ;;
259	  gate)
260	    static_checks
261	    other_parts
262	    dirs=$(test_dirs)
263	    # shellcheck disable=SC2086  # one directory per word, by construction
264	    [ -n "$dirs" ] && run_tests $dirs --no-cov -q -x
265	    step bash scripts/floor_guard.sh
266	    protected=()
267	    while IFS= read -r g; do protected+=("$g"); done < <(protected_globs)
268	    unchanged_since_base "protected files" "${protected[@]}"
269	    ;;
270	  full)
271	    static_checks
272	    other_parts
273	    if [ "$TYPE_GATE" = mypy ]; then run pyright; else run mypy; fi
274	    # CR-41: the interface task's stubs and fields stay unused until later tasks use them.
275	    if [ ${#exit_check[@]} -gt 0 ]; then run vulture; else report vulture; fi
276	    run pylint --disable=all --enable=duplicate-code --recursive=y --score=n --fail-under=10 "${SRC_DIRS[@]}"   # CR-42
277	    run typos
278	    run pip-audit --skip-editable   # the external opinion: a vulnerability database (CR-26)
279	    step bash scripts/floor_guard.sh ${exit_check[@]+"${exit_check[@]}"}
280	    dirs=$(test_dirs)
281	    # Coverage threshold comes from [tool.coverage.report] fail_under (CR-40).
282	    # shellcheck disable=SC2086
283	    [ -n "$dirs" ] && HYPOTHESIS_PROFILE=ci run_tests $dirs --cov -q
284	    ;;
285	  protected)
286	    protected_globs
287	    ;;
288	  *)
289	    echo "unknown mode: $mode (fast | collect | gate | full | protected)" >&2
290	    exit 64
291	    ;;
292	esac
293	[ $rc -ne 0 ] && printf 'FAILED: %s\n' "${failed[@]}" >&2
294	exit $rc

/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/plugin/dev-team/skills/architecture-design/references/contracts.md-10-- **Type sections.** Every type one step hands to another, and every type a public signature takes or returns, is its own section with the title `type`: `### profile.Profile: type`. The first line is its shape as code: a frozen dataclass's fields, a `typing.Protocol`, or a pandera schema's columns for a dataframe. Then **Invariants** and **Used by**. `pipeline-design` names these types in every step's `In → Out`; the build's interface task writes them, as code, into the owner's `types.py`. Freeze only what crosses: a helper's or a private function's types stay out.
/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/plugin/dev-team/skills/architecture-design/references/contracts.md-11-- **Contract first:** the contract is defined before either side is built. **The provider owns it.**
/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/plugin/dev-team/skills/architecture-design/references/contracts.md:12:- **Boundaries.** A symbol consumed across owners adds one line: `- **Boundary:** <consumers> · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/<id>.<symbol>/ · **Cases:** <the payloads a fixture must show>`. Every type that crosses step owners has one, components included: consumers are packages, components, or both. This section is the boundary's source of truth. `build-planning`'s interface task freezes it into that folder: a schema (JSON Schema across runtimes; a pandera schema for a dataframe in Python), one sample per case, and, for a function a pipeline calls from another pipeline or package, a fixture-backed fake of the provider. A Protocol or class type's case is a replay double (a fixture-backed recording of the real object's calls, `a recorded 5-batch epoch`), and so is a function another component's step calls: it is the type's sample, not a fake. Both sides then build at once against the same samples. A type needs at least one case (`dt lint` `type-cases`). Name the cases; don't write the fixtures. A case may name a probe's redacted sample (`a posted purchase (PB-01 samples/sync_added.json#0)`); the interface task derives the type's sample from it, never copies it.

/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/plugin/dev-team/skills/build-planning/references/phase0.md-25-
/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/plugin/dev-team/skills/build-planning/references/phase0.md-26-**b. Step spines** (`**Kind:** spine`, `**Step:** PL-NN.<function>`, solo, XS or S). One per new step, each depending only on the interface task: it replaces the stub naming it with the step's spine version from the step table. `Writes:` its owner module and that module's unit test, nothing else; steps sharing one module share one spine task. Acceptance: the step's input sample gives a schema-valid output; the signature is unchanged. A step another pipeline provides (`PL-01 as built`) gets no spine.
/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/plugin/dev-team/skills/build-planning/references/phase0.md-27-
/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/plugin/dev-team/skills/build-planning/references/phase0.md-28-**c. Wiring** (`**Kind:** wiring`, `**Step:** PL-NN`, solo). One per new pipeline: the entry function in the pipeline's own entry module (the layout's, never one module shared by several pipelines), so wiring tasks run side by side, and its smoke test. `__main__.py` is the interface task's. It depends on its pipeline's spines, since the smoke runs them (`order`, `none: integration: …`), never on their thicken tasks. A call to another pipeline or package runs against that boundary's fake, so wiring PL-02 never waits for PL-01. Acceptance: the smoke command runs end to end and is committed as the pipeline's smoke test; the pipeline's pre-build sections are replaced by a `dt layout` pointer (`dt land` runs `dt prebuild-clear` when the wiring lands).
/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/plugin/dev-team/skills/build-planning/references/phase0.md-29-
/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/plugin/dev-team/skills/build-planning/references/phase0.md-30-**d. System task** (`**Kind:** system`, solo, last). It swaps each fake for the real provider, in the entry its wiring wrote (a `file` edge), and runs every smoke command and the milestone's golden cases on the real chain; the boundary samples still validate against real output. Multi-package: one per new system pipeline, `**Step:**` that pipeline, `**Package:**` the owner of its first step, `**Reason:** system task`; the root has no pre-build sections, so no `dt layout` bullet. Single package: `**Step:**` the calling pipeline. A milestone with no fake has no system task.
/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/plugin/dev-team/skills/build-planning/references/phase0.md-31-

The pipeline's wiring depends on a fold task, so a fold is allowed only where that task is XS and the critical path doesn't grow. Record each on its task as `**Reason:** fold: <rule>`:

389	## Risks
390	| Risk | Impact | Mitigation |
391	|---|---|---|
392	| The online Q-learning spine doesn't solve the two-exit corridor | TC-01 fails and every batch is refused | TC-01 runs at the PL-01 wiring, before any batch; M2's DQN thicken adds replay and a target net |
393	| Seeding leaks between training and evaluation | TC-04 passes alone but runs differ once evaluations interleave | TC-04 runs with evaluations on; AD-05 keeps every generator spawned per episode |

145	## PL-08: Replay video
146	**Entry:** `src/rllab/cli/pipelines/pl08_video.py::run_video` · **Trigger:** `rllab video <world> <variant> <side> --seed N --out FILE [--sight]`
147	**Covers:** REPLAY-07, REPLAY-02, QR-06
148	
149	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
150	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
151	| 1 | `open_sources` | replay.sources | world, variant, side spec → Checkpoint or baseline per team | as `open_sides`, one side | REPLAY-07 |
152	| 2 | `make` | worlds.make | world, variant → World | PL-02 as built | WORLD-06 |
153	| 3 | `policies_for` | replay.sources | Checkpoint or baseline per team, WorldSpec → Policy per team | `methods.load_policy` or `baselines` per team; a team left out takes the first checkpoint's | REPLAY-01, REPLAY-06 |
154	| 4 | `play_episode` | core.episode | World, Policy per team, seed → Episode | reset, act per team, step until done or `max_steps`, frames kept | REPLAY-02, WORLD-02 |
155	| 5 | `to_trace` | replay.tracing | Episode, WorldSpec, label → Trace | copy frames, set label and step-limit flag | REPLAY-02 |
156	| 6 | `save_video` | replay.video | Trace, out → Output | draw each frame off-screen, imageio-ffmpeg H.264 at 30 fps | REPLAY-07, QR-06 |
157	
158	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
159	```python
160	def run_video(world: str, variant: str, side: str, seed: int, out: Path, *, overlay: bool = False) -> Output:
161	    sources = open_sources(world, variant, side)
162	    w = make(world, variant)
163	    policies = policies_for(sources, w.spec)
164	    episode = play_episode(w, policies, seed, trace=True)
165	    trace = to_trace(episode, w.spec, side)
166	    return save_video(trace, out, overlay_sight=overlay)
167	```

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ae4eb167c27df2d73/src/rllab/core/types.py
"""Shared contract types: action formats, world specs, steps, episodes, settings and seed sets.

Every other component imports these from ``rllab.core``; ``core`` imports nothing first-party.
"""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal, Protocol

import numpy as np
import numpy.typing as npt

type Direction = Literal["higher", "lower"]
type Settings = Mapping[str, int | float | str | bool]
type Array = npt.NDArray[Any]

EVAL_SEEDS: tuple[int, ...] = tuple(range(1_000_000, 1_000_020))
"""In-training evaluation seeds: 20, disjoint from training seeds and ``FINAL_SEEDS`` (TRAIN-04)."""

FINAL_SEEDS: tuple[int, ...] = tuple(range(2_000_000, 2_000_100))
"""Final evaluation seeds, shared by every run, baseline and reference (EVAL-03, CHECK-02)."""


class ActionError(ValueError):
    """An action outside the world's declared action format; worlds refuse it, never clip it."""

    def __init__(self, agent: str, value: object) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"action {value!r} for agent {agent!r} is outside the action format")
        self.agent = agent
        self.value = value


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Discrete:
    """Actions are ints in ``[0, n)``."""

    n: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Box:
    """Actions are ``dims`` floats, each in ``[low, high]``."""

    dims: int
    low: float
    high: float


type ActionSpace = Discrete | Box


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TeamSpec:
    """One team: its agents' indices on the world's agent axis and how its score reads."""

    name: str
    agents: tuple[int, ...]
    score_name: str
    direction: Direction


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MeasureSpec:
    """An extra per-episode measure a world declares for one team (LOG-05)."""

    name: str
    team: str
    direction: Direction


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class WorldSpec:
    """Everything a learner needs to know about a world, readable without an episode (WORLD-04)."""

    world: str
    variant: str
    agents: tuple[str, ...]
    teams: tuple[TeamSpec, ...]
    obs_len: int
    action: ActionSpace
    max_steps: int
    measures: tuple[MeasureSpec, ...]
    sight: float | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Step:
    """One world step: float32 ``obs`` (agents x obs_len), float32 ``rewards``, bool ``active``."""

    obs: Array
    rewards: Array
    active: Array
    done: bool
    truncated: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EpisodeResult:
    """One finished episode; ``scores`` and ``team_rewards`` keyed by team, ``measures`` by name."""

    seed: int
    length: int
    scores: Mapping[str, float]
    team_rewards: Mapping[str, float]
    measures: Mapping[str, float]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Drawable:
    """One object on screen; ``agent`` is set for agents so a viewer can draw their sight."""

    kind: str
    x: float
    y: float
    size: float
    team: str | None
    agent: int | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DrawState:
    """Every object on screen at one step (WORLD-06)."""

    width: float
    height: float
    objects: tuple[Drawable, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Frame:
    """One traced step: what to draw, each team's running score and the latest rewards."""

    step: int
    draw: DrawState
    scores: Mapping[str, float]
    rewards: Array


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Episode:
    """An episode's result, plus ``result.length + 1`` frames when traced."""

    result: EpisodeResult
    frames: tuple[Frame, ...] | None
    hit_step_limit: bool


class World(Protocol):
    """A seeded, deterministic world: same seed and actions give the same steps (WORLD-01, -02)."""

    spec: WorldSpec

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> Step: ...

    def step(self, actions: Array) -> Step: ...

    def result(self) -> EpisodeResult: ...

    def draw(self) -> DrawState: ...


class Policy(Protocol):
    """Maps one team's ``k x obs_len`` observations to ``k`` actions, deterministic per ``rng``."""

    def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array: ...


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Output:
    """A file a pipeline made, and the runs, checkpoints and seeds behind it (NOTES-02)."""

    path: Path
    kind: Literal["chart", "video", "writeup"]
    made_from: tuple[str, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MethodConfig:
    """One team's learning method and its full settings, defaults filled (AD-12)."""

    method: Literal["dqn", "ppo"]
    settings: Settings


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunConfig:
    """One training run: world, a method per team, seed and world-step budget (AD-12)."""

    name: str
    world: str
    variant: str
    methods: Mapping[str, MethodConfig]
    seed: int
    budget: int
    check_settings: Mapping[str, Settings]
    device: str = "cpu"
full input and output

"""The one episode loop every component plays through."""

from collections.abc import Mapping

from rllab.core.types import Episode, Policy, World


def play_episode(
    world: World, policies: Mapping[str, Policy], seed: int, *, trace: bool = False
) -> Episode:
    """Play one episode of ``world`` from ``seed``, one policy per team, frames kept if ``trace``.

    The seed spawns the world's and each policy's generator (AD-05); the episode stops at
    ``done`` or ``spec.max_steps``. Raises ``KeyError`` naming a team with no policy.
    """
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T18")

297	def _exempt_functions(tree: ast.Module) -> set[ast.AST]:
298	    """Abstract methods, overloads and Protocol members may have placeholder bodies."""
299	    exempt: set[ast.AST] = set()
300	    for node in ast.walk(tree):
301	        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
302	            if {_name(d) for d in node.decorator_list} & EXEMPT_DECORATORS:
303	                exempt.add(node)
304	        elif isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef) and "Protocol" in {_name(b) for b in node.bases}:
305	            exempt.update(
306	                n for n in node.body if isinstance(n, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef))
307	            )
308	    return exempt

311	def _in_folder(name: str | None, folder: str) -> bool:
312	    return name is not None and (name == folder or name.startswith(f"{folder}."))
313

315	def _mentions(node: ast.AST, word: re.Pattern[str]) -> bool:
316	    return any(
317	        isinstance(n, ast.Constant) and isinstance(n.value, str) and word.search(n.value)
318	        for n in ast.walk(node)
319	    )

322	def _is_sys_path(node: ast.AST) -> bool:
323	    return (
324	        isinstance(node, ast.Attribute)
325	        and node.attr == "path"
326	        and isinstance(node.value, ast.Name)
327	        and node.value.id == "sys"
328	    )
329	
330	
331	def _imported_modules(node: ast.AST) -> list[str | None]:
332	    """Absolute module names an import statement or an import_module call brings in."""
333	    if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
334	        return [alias.name for alias in node.names]
335	    if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
336	        return [node.module] if node.level == 0 else []
337	    if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and _name(node.func) in {"import_module", "__import__"}:
338	        first = node.args[0] if node.args else None
339	        if isinstance(first, ast.Constant) and isinstance(first.value, str):
340	            return [first.value]
341	    return []
342	
343	
344	def _changes_sys_path(node: ast.AST) -> bool:
345	    """sys.path.insert/append/extend, site.addsitedir, or an assignment to sys.path."""
346	    if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and isinstance(node.func, ast.Attribute):
347	        func = node.func
348	        return func.attr == "addsitedir" or (
349	            func.attr in SYS_PATH_CALLS and _is_sys_path(func.value)
350	        )
351	    if isinstance(node, ast.Assign):
352	        return any(_is_sys_path(target) for target in node.targets)
353	    if isinstance(node, ast.AugAssign):
354	        return _is_sys_path(node.target)
355	    return False
356	
357	
358	def _outside_finding(path: str, node: ast.AST) -> Finding | None:
359	    """CR-08: an import from references/ or probes/, or either folder added to sys.path."""
360	    line = getattr(node, "lineno", 0)
361	    for folder, word in OUTSIDE.items():
362	        if any(_in_folder(name, folder) for name in _imported_modules(node)):
363	            return Finding(f"{folder}-import", path, line, f"import from {folder}/")
364	        if _changes_sys_path(node) and _mentions(node, word):
365	            return Finding(f"{folder}-path", path, line, f"{folder}/ added to sys.path")
366	    return None
367	
368	
369	def _asserts(func: ast.AST) -> bool:
370	    for node in ast.walk(func):
371	        if isinstance(node, ast.Assert):
372	            return True
373	        name = _name(node) if isinstance(node, ast.Call) else None
374	        if name and (
375	            name.startswith("assert") or name in {"raises", "warns", "deprecated_call", "fail"}
376	        ):
377	            return True
378	    return False
379	
380	
381	def ast_findings(path: str, lines: set[int]) -> list[Finding]:
382	    try:
383	        tree = ast.parse(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
384	    except (SyntaxError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
385	        return []  # ruff reports syntax errors; a deleted file has nothing to check
386	    exempt, test = _exempt_functions(tree), is_test_file(path)
387	    found: list[Finding] = []
388	    for node in ast.walk(tree):
389	        if not _touches(node, lines):
390	            continue
391	        if outside := _outside_finding(path, node):
392	            found.append(outside)
393	        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
394	            if (
395	                node not in exempt
396	                and not path.endswith(".pyi")
397	                and _is_trivial(node.body)
398	                and not _task_stub(node.body)
399	            ):
400	                found.append(
401	                    Finding("stub", path, node.lineno, f"`{node.name}` has a placeholder body")
402	                )
403	            if test and node.name.startswith("test") and not _asserts(node):
404	                found.append(
405	                    Finding(
406	                        "test-without-assertion",
407	                        path,
408	                        node.lineno,
409	                        f"`{node.name}` asserts nothing",
410	                    )
411	                )
412	        elif isinstance(node, ast.ExceptHandler) and _is_trivial(node.body):
413	            if (None if node.type is None else _name(node.type)) in TRIVIAL_EXCEPT:
414	                found.append(
415	                    Finding("swallowed-exception", path, node.lineno, "exception swallowed")
416	                )
417	    return found

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ae4eb167c27df2d73/src/rllab/runlog/types.py
"""Contract types the runlog component owns. Every field type comes from ``core`` (AD-12)."""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal, Protocol

from rllab.core import EpisodeResult, MethodConfig, RunConfig, WorldSpec


class RunExistsError(FileExistsError):
    """A run folder with this name already exists; nothing was written."""

    def __init__(self, path: Path) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"run folder already exists: {path}")
        self.path = path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Failure:
    """Where a run stopped on an invalid number, and its last good checkpoint."""

    step: int
    value: float
    checkpoint: Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunRecord:
    """A run's ``run.json``: settings, world spec, timing, status and any failure (LOG-01)."""

    config: RunConfig
    spec: WorldSpec
    started: datetime
    ended: datetime | None
    status: Literal["running", "finished", "incomplete", "failed"]
    failure: Failure | None
    path: Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EvalPoint:
    """Mean scores per team and per measure over the 20 ``EVAL_SEEDS`` episodes at one step."""

    step: int
    scores: Mapping[str, float]
    measures: Mapping[str, float]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Checkpoint:
    """One saved checkpoint: ``pct`` in {0, 10, ..., 100}, ``state`` keyed by team."""

    run: str
    pct: int
    step: int
    spec: WorldSpec
    methods: Mapping[str, MethodConfig]
    state: Mapping[str, dict[str, object]]
    path: Path


class Run(Protocol):
    """A read-only view of one run folder, readable while the run writes (LOG-06)."""

    record: RunRecord

    def episodes(self) -> list[EpisodeResult]: ...

    def evals(self) -> list[EvalPoint]: ...

    def checkpoints(self) -> list[Checkpoint]: ...


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Progress:
    """A live run's snapshot, at most one evaluation point behind it (LOG-06)."""

    step: int
    budget: int
    latest: EvalPoint | None
    elapsed: timedelta
    status: str

"""The run folder writer."""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core import EpisodeResult, RunConfig, WorldSpec
from rllab.runlog.types import Checkpoint, EvalPoint, Failure, RunRecord


class RunWriter:
    """Writes ``runs/<world>/<variant>/<name>``: record, episode and eval logs, checkpoints.

    Raises ``RunExistsError`` naming the path if the run folder exists.
    """

    def __init__(self, root: Path, config: RunConfig, spec: WorldSpec) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

    def episode(self, result: EpisodeResult) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

    def eval(self, point: EvalPoint) -> None:
        """Append one evaluation point and flush every log."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

    def checkpoint(self, pct: int, step: int, state: Mapping[str, dict[str, object]]) -> Checkpoint:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

    def close(self, status: str, failure: Failure | None = None) -> RunRecord:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

"""Contract types the methods component owns."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Protocol

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Array, Policy


class NonFiniteError(ArithmeticError):
    """A loss or parameter became NaN or infinite; raised before any parameter changed."""

    def __init__(self, value: float) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"non-finite value {value!r} in a loss or parameter")
        self.value = value


class SpecMismatchError(ValueError):
    """A checkpoint's world spec differs from the world it is asked to play."""

    def __init__(self, fields: tuple[str, ...]) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"checkpoint spec differs from the world in: {', '.join(fields)}")
        self.fields = fields


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Transition:
    """One step for one team; rows are its agents in ``TeamSpec.agents`` order."""

    obs: Array
    actions: Array
    rewards: Array
    next_obs: Array
    done: bool
    truncated: bool
    active: Array


class Learner(Protocol):
    """A hand-written learner for one team (CHECK-01)."""

    def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array:
        """Exploring actions for the team's agents."""
        ...

    def observe(self, t: Transition) -> None:
        """Take one transition; updates on its own schedule. May raise ``NonFiniteError``."""
        ...

    def policy(self) -> Policy:
        """A frozen, deterministic copy: greedy (DQN) or the mean action (PPO)."""
        ...

    def state_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]: ...

"""Contract types the train component owns."""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CheckResult:
    """One method's check-world result; ``passed`` is ``agreement >= 0.95`` (TRAIN-06)."""

    method: str
    world: str
    agreement: float
    passed: bool


class CheckFailedError(RuntimeError):
    """A method failed its check world, so no run of the batch starts."""

    def __init__(self, result: CheckResult) -> None:
        super().__init__(
            f"method check failed: {result.method} on {result.world} "
            f"agreed on {result.agreement:.0%} of states (needs 95%)"
        )
        self.result = result

from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core import RunConfig, World
from rllab.methods import Learner
from rllab.runlog import RunRecord


def train_loop(
    world: World, learners: Mapping[str, Learner], config: RunConfig, root: Path
) -> RunRecord:
    """Step ``world`` to ``config.budget``, writing the run folder under ``root``.

    Checkpoints at 0, 10, ..., 100%; evaluations on ``EVAL_SEEDS`` at every 5%; a
    ``NonFiniteError`` ends the run as ``failed``.
    """
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

"""Contract types the charts component owns."""


class MixedRunsError(ValueError):
    """The runs to chart differ by more than their seed."""

    def __init__(self, settings: tuple[str, ...]) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"runs differ by more than seed, in: {', '.join(settings)}")
        self.settings = settings

"""Contract types the evaluate component owns."""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Comparison:
    """One learned-vs-baseline test; ``difference`` is positive when the learned agent is better."""

    baseline: Literal["random", "rule"]
    team: str
    learned_mean: float
    baseline_mean: float
    difference: float
    p_raw: float
    p_holm: float
    rejected: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Verdict:
    """Whether the team beats both baselines (EVAL-04, EVAL-05), and why not."""

    beats_baselines: bool
    failing_runs: tuple[str, ...]
    runs_needed: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Summary:
    """The final evaluation of one world, variant and team (EVAL-06, PREY-08)."""

    world: str
    variant: str
    team: str
    runs: tuple[str, ...]
    learned_mean: float
    learned_range: tuple[float, float]
    run_means: Mapping[str, float]
    random_mean: float
    rule_mean: float
    comparisons: tuple[Comparison, ...]
    verdict: Verdict
    collapse: int | None

from rllab.core import Direction

def verdict(
    run_means: Mapping[str, float],

    comparisons: Sequence[Comparison],
    direction: Direction,
) -> Verdict:

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ae4eb167c27df2d73/src/rllab/cli/dispatch.py
"""The ``rllab`` command: each subcommand dispatched to its pipeline entry (AD-10)."""

import argparse
import sys
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.cli.pipelines.pl04_watch import run_watch
from rllab.cli.pipelines.pl05_chart import run_chart
from rllab.cli.pipelines.pl06_evaluate import run_evaluate
from rllab.train import run_batch, run_check


def parse_seeds(text: str) -> list[int]:
    """Seeds from ``"1-5"`` (inclusive range) or ``"1,3,4"``."""
    first, dash, last = text.partition("-")
    if dash:
        return list(range(int(first), int(last) + 1))
    return [int(part) for part in text.split(",")]


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="rllab", description="RL Lab: train, watch, evaluate.")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)

    check = sub.add_parser("check", help="run the method check of a config")
    check.add_argument("config", type=Path)
    check.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)

    train = sub.add_parser("train", help="train one seed of a config (a one-seed batch)")
    train.add_argument("config", type=Path)
    train.add_argument("--seed", type=int, required=True)

    batch = sub.add_parser("batch", help="train several seeds of a config in parallel")
    batch.add_argument("config", type=Path)
    batch.add_argument("--seeds", type=parse_seeds, required=True)
    batch.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=None)

    watch = sub.add_parser("watch", help="show a run's progress")
    watch.add_argument("run_path", type=Path)

    chart = sub.add_parser("chart", help="draw the learning curve of a world's runs")
    chart.add_argument("world")
    chart.add_argument("variant")
    chart.add_argument("--measure", default=None)
    chart.add_argument("--include", action="append", default=[])

    evaluate = sub.add_parser("evaluate", help="final evaluation of a world's runs")
    evaluate.add_argument("world")
    evaluate.add_argument("variant")
    evaluate.add_argument("--include", action="append", default=[])
    evaluate.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=None)

    for command in (train, batch, chart, evaluate):
        command.add_argument("--root", type=Path, default=Path())
    return parser


def dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    """Call the pipeline entry the subcommand names and return its result."""
    match args.command:
        case "check":
            return run_check(args.config, args.seed)
        case "train":
            return run_batch(args.config, [args.seed], args.root)
        case "batch":
            return run_batch(args.config, args.seeds, args.root, workers=args.workers)
        case "watch":
            return run_watch(args.run_path)
        case "chart":
            return run_chart(
                args.root, args.world, args.variant, include=args.include, measure=args.measure
            )
        case _:
            return run_evaluate(
                args.root, args.world, args.variant, include=args.include, workers=args.workers
            )


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    """Run one ``rllab`` subcommand, print its result; any error is one line and exit code 1."""
    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:
        result = dispatch(args)
    except Exception as error:  # every failure is reported as one line (cli.main contract)
        print(f"rllab {args.command}: {type(error).__name__}: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    for item in result if isinstance(result, list) else [result]:
        print(item)
    return 0

"""The ``rllab`` command: each subcommand dispatched to its pipeline entry (AD-10)."""

import argparse
import sys
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.cli.pipelines.pl04_watch import run_watch
from rllab.cli.pipelines.pl05_chart import run_chart
from rllab.cli.pipelines.pl06_evaluate import run_evaluate
from rllab.train import run_batch, run_check


def parse_seeds(text: str) -> list[int]:
    """Seeds from ``"1-5"`` (inclusive range) or ``"1,3,4"``."""
    first, dash, last = text.partition("-")
    if dash:
        return list(range(int(first), int(last) + 1))
    return [int(part) for part in text.split(",")]


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    """One subparser per trigger; each sets ``entry``, the call to its pipeline entry."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="rllab", description="RL Lab: train, watch, evaluate.")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)

    check = sub.add_parser("check", help="run the method check of a config")
    check.add_argument("config", type=Path)
    check.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    check.set_defaults(entry=lambda a: run_check(a.config, a.seed))

    train = sub.add_parser("train", help="train one seed of a config (a one-seed batch)")
    train.add_argument("config", type=Path)
    train.add_argument("--seed", type=int, required=True)
    train.set_defaults(entry=lambda a: run_batch(a.config, [a.seed], a.root))

    batch = sub.add_parser("batch", help="train several seeds of a config in parallel")
    batch.add_argument("config", type=Path)
    batch.add_argument("--seeds", type=parse_seeds, required=True)
    batch.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=None)
    batch.set_defaults(entry=lambda a: run_batch(a.config, a.seeds, a.root, workers=a.workers))

    watch = sub.add_parser("watch", help="show a run's progress")
    watch.add_argument("run_path", type=Path)
    watch.set_defaults(entry=lambda a: run_watch(a.run_path))

    chart = sub.add_parser("chart", help="draw the learning curve of a world's runs")
    chart.add_argument("world")
    chart.add_argument("variant")
    chart.add_argument("--measure", default=None)
    chart.add_argument("--include", action="append", default=[])
    chart.set_defaults(
        entry=lambda a: run_chart(a.root, a.world, a.variant, include=a.include, measure=a.measure)
    )

    evaluate = sub.add_parser("evaluate", help="final evaluation of a world's runs")
    evaluate.add_argument("world")
    evaluate.add_argument("variant")
    evaluate.add_argument("--include", action="append", default=[])
    evaluate.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=None)
    evaluate.set_defaults(
        entry=lambda a: run_evaluate(
            a.root, a.world, a.variant, include=a.include, workers=a.workers
        )
    )

    for command in (train, batch, chart, evaluate):
        command.add_argument("--root", type=Path, default=Path(), help="folder holding runs/")
    return parser


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    """Run one ``rllab`` subcommand, print its result; any error is one line and exit code 1."""
    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:
        result = args.entry(args)
    except Exception as error:  # every failure is reported as one line (cli.main contract)
        print(f"rllab {args.command}: {type(error).__name__}: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    for item in result if isinstance(result, list) else [result]:
        print(item)
    return 0
