"""Fake of ``evaluate.final_evaluation``: the passing snake summary."""

from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.evaluate import Summary
from rllab.runlog import Run
from tests.contracts.contracts import cases, raw, sample


def final_evaluation(
    runs: Sequence[Run], out: Path, *, workers: int | None = None
) -> list[Summary]:
    """The recorded summaries for exactly these runs (nothing is played or written);
    ``LookupError`` for any other set of runs."""
    names = [run.record.config.name for run in runs]
    for case in cases("evaluate.final_evaluation"):
        fixture = raw("evaluate.final_evaluation", case)
        if names == fixture["runs"]:
            return [sample(r["sample"], r["case"]) for r in fixture["returns"]]
    raise LookupError(f"no recorded final evaluation for runs {names}")

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ae4eb167c27df2d73/tests/contracts/test_samples.py
"""Every contract sample validates against its schema and builds into its Python type."""

import typing

import numpy as np
import pytest

from rllab import core
from tests.contracts.contracts import (
    build,
    cases,
    folders,
    python_object,
    raw,
    sample,
    schema,
    to_json,
    validate,
)

# The M1 boundary symbols with a Fixtures folder (contract.md); each must have one.
SYMBOLS = {
    "core.ActionSpace", "core.TeamSpec", "core.WorldSpec", "core.Step", "core.EpisodeResult",
    "core.DrawState", "core.Frame", "core.Episode", "core.World", "core.Policy",
    "core.play_episode", "core.EVAL_SEEDS", "core.FINAL_SEEDS", "core.Output",
    "core.MethodConfig", "core.RunConfig", "worlds.make", "worlds.CheckWorld",
    "baselines.RandomPolicy", "baselines.RulePolicy", "baselines.rule_for",
    "baselines.BaselineMeans", "baselines.baseline_means", "methods.Transition",
    "methods.Learner", "methods.make_learner", "methods.load_policy",
    "train.load_run_configs", "train.run_check", "train.run_train", "train.run_batch",
    "train.CheckResult", "runlog.RunRecord", "runlog.EvalPoint", "runlog.Checkpoint",
    "runlog.Run", "runlog.RunWriter", "runlog.find_runs", "runlog.open_run", "runlog.Progress",
    "runlog.progress", "charts.learning_curve", "evaluate.Comparison", "evaluate.Summary",
    "evaluate.final_evaluation",
}  # fmt: skip
DOUBLES = {"core.World", "core.Policy", "worlds.CheckWorld", "baselines.RulePolicy",
           "methods.Learner", "runlog.Run", "runlog.RunWriter"}  # fmt: skip
FAKES = {"core.play_episode", "worlds.make", "baselines.rule_for", "baselines.baseline_means",
         "methods.make_learner", "methods.load_policy", "train.load_run_configs",
         "train.run_check", "train.run_train", "train.run_batch", "runlog.find_runs",
         "runlog.open_run", "runlog.progress", "charts.learning_curve",
         "evaluate.final_evaluation"}  # fmt: skip
ALL_CASES = [(f.name, case) for f in folders() for case in cases(f.name)]
TYPED_CASES = [(s, c) for s, c in ALL_CASES if "title" in schema(s)]


def test_every_boundary_symbol_has_its_folder_and_nothing_else() -> None:
    assert {f.name for f in folders()} == SYMBOLS


@pytest.mark.parametrize("symbol", sorted(SYMBOLS))
def test_folder_has_a_schema_samples_and_its_double_or_fake(symbol: str) -> None:
    files = {p.name for p in (folders()[0].parent / symbol).iterdir()}
    assert {"schema.json", "samples"} <= files
    assert cases(symbol), f"{symbol} has no sample"
    assert ("double.py" in files) == (symbol in DOUBLES)
    assert ("fake.py" in files) == (symbol in FAKES)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("symbol", "case"), ALL_CASES)
def test_sample_validates_against_its_schema(symbol: str, case: str) -> None:
    assert validate(raw(symbol, case), schema(symbol)) == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("symbol", "case"), TYPED_CASES)
def test_typed_sample_builds_and_round_trips(symbol: str, case: str) -> None:
    target = python_object(schema(symbol)["title"])
    if isinstance(target, (type, typing.TypeAliasType)):
        built = sample(symbol, case)
        assert validate(to_json(built), schema(symbol)) == []
        assert to_json(built) == to_json(raw(symbol, case)) or symbol == "runlog.RunRecord"
    else:  # a constant: the sample is its value
        assert tuple(raw(symbol, case)) == target


def test_seed_sets_are_disjoint_from_training_seeds_and_each_other() -> None:
    assert len(core.EVAL_SEEDS) == 20
    assert len(core.FINAL_SEEDS) == 100
    assert min(core.EVAL_SEEDS) >= 1_000_000
    assert not set(core.EVAL_SEEDS) & set(core.FINAL_SEEDS)


def test_failed_record_keeps_its_nan_value() -> None:
    failed = sample("runlog.RunRecord", "failed-nan")
    assert failed.status == "failed"
    assert failed.failure is not None
    assert np.isnan(failed.failure.value)
    assert failed.failure.step == 41_200


def test_samples_follow_the_type_invariants() -> None:
    spec = sample("core.WorldSpec", "prey-default")
    agents = sorted(i for team in spec.teams for i in team.agents)
    assert agents == list(range(len(spec.agents)))
    caught = sample("core.Step", "prey-one-caught")
    assert caught.obs.shape == (len(spec.agents), spec.obs_len)
    assert caught.obs.dtype == np.float32
    assert not caught.active[2]
    assert not caught.obs[2].any()
    assert caught.rewards[2] == 0
    traced = sample("core.Episode", "check-discrete-traced-3-steps")
    assert traced.frames is not None
    assert len(traced.frames) == traced.result.length + 1
    assert sample("core.Episode", "snake-untraced").frames is None
    for case in cases("runlog.Checkpoint"):
        assert sample("runlog.Checkpoint", case).pct in range(0, 101, 10)
    check = sample("train.CheckResult", "ppo-fail")
    assert check.passed == (check.agreement >= 0.95)


def test_summary_samples_follow_the_verdict_rules() -> None:
    three = sample("evaluate.Summary", "snake-3-runs")
    assert three.comparisons == ()
    assert three.verdict.runs_needed == 5 - len(three.runs)
    failing = sample("evaluate.Summary", "snake-failing-run")
    below = tuple(n for n, m in failing.run_means.items() if m <= failing.rule_mean)
    assert failing.verdict.failing_runs == below
    assert not failing.verdict.beats_baselines
    passing = sample("evaluate.Summary", "snake-pass")
    assert passing.verdict.beats_baselines
    assert all(c.rejected for c in passing.comparisons)
    assert passing.learned_range == (min(passing.run_means.values()), max(passing.run_means.values()))


def test_action_space_samples_build_into_their_variant() -> None:
    assert sample("core.ActionSpace", "snake-discrete") == core.Discrete(3)
    assert sample("core.ActionSpace", "fish-box") == core.Box(2, -1.0, 1.0)


def test_build_refuses_a_sample_that_does_not_fit() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(TypeError, match="no fields"):
        build(core.TeamSpec, raw("core.TeamSpec", "snake") | {"colour": "red"})
    with pytest.raises(TypeError, match="not one of"):
        build(core.TeamSpec, raw("core.TeamSpec", "snake") | {"direction": "up"})


def test_validator_rejects_wrong_types_missing_fields_and_unknown_keywords() -> None:
    team_schema = schema("core.TeamSpec")
    bad = raw("core.TeamSpec", "snake") | {"agents": ["0"]}
    assert validate(bad, team_schema)
    missing = {k: v for k, v in raw("core.TeamSpec", "snake").items() if k != "name"}
    assert validate(missing, team_schema) == ["$: missing 'name'"]
    assert validate(1, {"type": "integer", "multipleOf": 2}) != []
    assert validate(True, {"type": "integer"}) != []

"""Fake of ``runlog.progress``: the 35% snake progress sample.

``runlog.progress`` shares this folder with ``runlog.Progress``: ``runlog.progress/`` and
``runlog.Progress/`` are one folder on a case-insensitive filesystem. Load it with
``fake("runlog.Progress")``.
"""

from pathlib import Path

from rllab.runlog import Progress
from tests.contracts.contracts import sample

# The run folder name the fake serves, and the Progress sample it returns for it.
SERVES = {"snake-default-live": "snake-35pct"}


def progress(path: Path) -> Progress:
    """The ``Progress`` sample served for ``path.name``; ``FileNotFoundError`` otherwise."""
    if path.name not in SERVES:
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"no run at {path}")
    snapshot: Progress = sample("runlog.Progress", SERVES[path.name])
    return snapshot

    def _build_generic(tp: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
        origin, args = typing.get_origin(tp), typing.get_args(tp)

def _build_generic(tp: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
new
def _unalias(tp: Any) -> Any:
    """Resolve ``type`` aliases, including a subscripted one such as ``NDArray[Any]``."""
    while True:
        if isinstance(tp, typing.TypeAliasType):
            tp = tp.__value__
        elif isinstance(typing.get_origin(tp), typing.TypeAliasType):
            tp = typing.get_origin(tp).__value__
        else:
            return tp

"""Each Protocol's replay double serves its recording, and refuses what it didn't record."""

import numpy as np
import pytest
from tests.contracts.contracts import build, double, raw, same, to_array

from rllab.core import ActionError, DrawState, EpisodeResult, Step
from rllab.methods import Transition
from rllab.runlog import Checkpoint, EvalPoint

RNG = np.random.default_rng(0)


def test_world_double_replays_the_seed_42_episode() -> None:
    world = double("core.World").ReplayWorld()
    recording = raw("core.World", "snake-seed42")
    assert world.spec.world == "snake"
    first = world.reset(42)
    assert same(first, build(Step, recording["reset"]))
    assert first.obs.shape == (1, world.spec.obs_len)
    assert world.draw().objects[0].kind == "snake-head"
    steps = [world.step(np.array(s["actions"])) for s in recording["steps"]]
    assert [s.done for s in steps] == [False] * 4 + [True]
    assert sum(float(s.rewards.sum()) for s in steps) == world.result().team_rewards["snake"]
    assert isinstance(world.draw(), DrawState)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="after the episode ended"):
        world.step(np.array([2]))


def test_world_double_refuses_unrecorded_seeds_and_actions() -> None:
    world = double("core.World").ReplayWorld()
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="recorded seed 42"):
        world.reset(7)
    world.reset(42)
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="recorded actions"):
        world.step(np.array([1]))
    with pytest.raises(ActionError) as caught:
        world.step(np.array([3]))
    assert (caught.value.agent, caught.value.value) == ("snake", 3)


def test_loose_world_double_serves_any_seed_and_in_format_actions() -> None:
    world = double("core.World").ReplayWorld(strict=False)
    for seed in (1_000_000, 7):
        world.reset(seed)
        steps = [world.step(np.array([1])) for _ in range(5)]
        assert steps[-1].done
    assert isinstance(world.result(), EpisodeResult)


def test_policy_double_replays_its_act_calls() -> None:
    policy = double("core.Policy").ReplayPolicy()
    calls = raw("core.Policy", "snake-rule-5-obs")["calls"]
    actions = [policy.act(to_array(c["obs"]), RNG) for c in calls]
    assert [a.tolist() for a in actions] == [c["actions"] for c in calls]
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="only 5 act calls"):
        policy.act(to_array(calls[0]["obs"]), RNG)


def test_policy_double_refuses_an_unrecorded_observation_unless_loose() -> None:
    calls = raw("core.Policy", "snake-rule-5-obs")["calls"]
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="obs differs"):
        double("core.Policy").ReplayPolicy().act(to_array(calls[3]["obs"]), RNG)
    loose = double("core.Policy").ReplayPolicy(strict=False)
    assert loose.act(to_array(calls[3]["obs"]), RNG).tolist() == calls[3]["actions"]
    assert loose.act(np.zeros((1, 10), np.float32), RNG).shape == (1,)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", ["corridor", "target-point"])
def test_check_world_double_serves_states_best_actions_and_its_episode(case: str) -> None:
    world = double("worlds.CheckWorld").ReplayCheckWorld(case)
    recording = raw("worlds.CheckWorld", case)
    assert world.states().shape == (6, world.spec.obs_len)
    assert len(world.best_actions()) == len(world.states())
    world.reset(recording["seed"])
    for s in recording["steps"]:
        assert same(world.step(to_array(s["actions"])), build(Step, s["step"]))


def test_check_world_double_refuses_out_of_format_actions() -> None:
    world = double("worlds.CheckWorld").ReplayCheckWorld("target-point")
    world.reset(0)
    with pytest.raises(ActionError):
        world.step(np.array([[1.5]]))
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="has not ended"):
        world.result()


def test_rule_double_has_a_one_sentence_description_and_replays() -> None:
    for case in ("snake-rule-5-obs", "fish-rule-no-ball"):
        rule = double("baselines.RulePolicy").ReplayRulePolicy(case)
        assert rule.description.endswith(".")
        assert rule.description.count(". ") == 0
        for call in raw("baselines.RulePolicy", case)["calls"]:
            assert rule.act(to_array(call["obs"]), RNG).tolist() == call["actions"]


def test_learner_double_replays_its_transitions_policy_and_state() -> None:
    learner = double("methods.Learner").ReplayLearner()
    recording = raw("methods.Learner", "dqn-snake-10-transitions")
    for act, t in zip(recording["acts"], recording["transitions"], strict=True):
        assert learner.act(to_array(act["obs"]), RNG).tolist() == act["actions"]
        learner.observe(build(Transition, t))
    assert learner.observed == 10
    frozen = learner.policy()
    first = recording["policy"]["calls"][0]
    assert frozen.act(to_array(first["obs"]), RNG).tolist() == first["actions"]
    assert set(learner.state_dict()) == {"q.0.weight", "q.0.bias", "q.2.weight", "q.2.bias"}


def test_learner_double_refuses_an_unrecorded_transition_unless_loose() -> None:
    recording = raw("methods.Learner", "dqn-snake-10-transitions")
    second = build(Transition, recording["transitions"][1])
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="transition 0 differs"):
        double("methods.Learner").ReplayLearner().observe(second)
    loose = double("methods.Learner").ReplayLearner(strict=False)
    for _ in range(25):
        loose.observe(second)
    assert loose.observed == 25


def test_run_double_serves_a_finished_three_checkpoint_run() -> None:
    run = double("runlog.Run").ReplayRun("snake-s1")
    assert run.record.status == "finished"
    assert [c.pct for c in run.checkpoints()] == [0, 50, 100]
    assert all(isinstance(c, Checkpoint) for c in run.checkpoints())
    evals = run.evals()
    assert [e.step for e in evals] == list(range(0, 2001, 100))
    assert all(isinstance(e, EvalPoint) for e in evals)
    assert sum(e.length for e in run.episodes()) <= run.record.config.budget


def test_run_double_serves_the_incomplete_run() -> None:
    run = double("runlog.Run").ReplayRun("snake-s6-incomplete")
    assert run.record.status == "incomplete"
    assert run.record.ended is None
    assert run.evals()[-1].step == 900


def test_run_writer_double_replays_the_writes_of_a_run(tmp_path: object) -> None:
    recording = raw("runlog.RunWriter", "snake-s1-writes")
    writer = _writer(recording, strict=True)
    for call in recording["calls"]:
        args = _args(call)
        returned = getattr(writer, call["method"])(**args)
        if call["method"] in {"checkpoint", "close"}:
            assert same(returned, build(type(returned), call["returns"]))
    assert len(writer.calls) == len(recording["calls"])
    assert writer.calls[-1][0] == "close"


def test_run_writer_double_refuses_calls_out_of_order() -> None:
    recording = raw("runlog.RunWriter", "snake-s1-writes")
    writer = _writer(recording, strict=True)
    eval_call = next(c for c in recording["calls"] if c["method"] == "eval")
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="recorded checkpoint"):
        writer.eval(**_args(eval_call))


def test_loose_run_writer_double_serves_checkpoints_by_pct() -> None:
    recording = raw("runlog.RunWriter", "snake-s1-writes")
    writer = _writer(recording, strict=False)
    assert writer.checkpoint(100, 2000, {"snake": {}}).pct == 100
    with pytest.raises(LookupError, match="10%"):
        writer.checkpoint(10, 200, {"snake": {}})
    assert writer.close("incomplete").status == "incomplete"


def _writer(recording: dict, *, strict: bool) -> object:
    from pathlib import Path

    from rllab.core import RunConfig, WorldSpec

    config = build(RunConfig, recording["config"])
    spec = build(WorldSpec, recording["spec"])
    return double("runlog.RunWriter").ReplayRunWriter(Path(), config, spec, strict=strict)


def _args(call: dict) -> dict:
    types = double("runlog.RunWriter").ARGUMENT_TYPES[call["method"]]
    return {k: build(types[k], v) for k, v in call["args"].items()}

"""Each boundary function's fake serves its fixtures, with the contract's signature and errors."""

import inspect
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pytest
import torch
from tests.contracts.contracts import double, fake, sample

from rllab import baselines, charts, core, evaluate, methods, runlog, train, worlds
from rllab.methods import SpecMismatchError
from rllab.runlog import RunExistsError

RNG = np.random.default_rng(0)
REAL = {
    "core.play_episode": core.play_episode,
    "worlds.make": worlds.make,
    "baselines.rule_for": baselines.rule_for,
    "baselines.baseline_means": baselines.baseline_means,
    "methods.make_learner": methods.make_learner,
    "methods.load_policy": methods.load_policy,
    "train.load_run_configs": train.load_run_configs,
    "train.run_check": train.run_check,
    "train.run_train": train.run_train,
    "train.run_batch": train.run_batch,
    "runlog.find_runs": runlog.find_runs,
    "runlog.open_run": runlog.open_run,
    "runlog.Progress": runlog.progress,
    "charts.learning_curve": charts.learning_curve,
    "evaluate.final_evaluation": evaluate.final_evaluation,
}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("symbol", sorted(REAL))
def test_fake_has_the_contract_signature(symbol: str) -> None:
    real = REAL[symbol]
    stand_in = getattr(fake(symbol), real.__name__)
    assert list(inspect.signature(stand_in).parameters) == list(
        inspect.signature(real).parameters
    )


def test_play_episode_fake_serves_the_traced_seed_7_episode() -> None:
    play = fake("core.play_episode").play_episode
    world = double("core.World").ReplayWorld()
    rule = double("baselines.RulePolicy").ReplayRulePolicy()
    traced = play(world, {"snake": rule}, 7, trace=True)
    assert traced.result.seed == 7
    assert traced.frames is not None
    assert len(traced.frames) == traced.result.length + 1
    assert play(world, {"snake": rule}, 7).frames is None
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="snake"):
        play(world, {}, 7)
    with pytest.raises(LookupError, match="seed 8"):
        play(world, {"snake": rule}, 8)


def test_make_fake_serves_the_seed_42_snake_double() -> None:
    make = fake("worlds.make").make
    world = make("snake")
    assert world.spec == sample("core.WorldSpec", "snake-default")
    assert world.reset(42).obs.shape == (1, 10)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unknown world 'chess'"):
        make("chess")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unknown variant 'huge'"):
        make("snake", "huge")


def test_rule_for_fake_serves_the_snake_rule() -> None:
    rule_for = fake("baselines.rule_for").rule_for
    assert rule_for("snake", "default", "snake").description.startswith("Moves toward the food")
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="fish"):
        rule_for("fish", "one", "fish")


def test_baseline_means_fake_serves_the_snake_sample() -> None:
    means = fake("baselines.baseline_means").baseline_means("snake", "default")
    assert means == sample("baselines.BaselineMeans", "snake")
    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        fake("baselines.baseline_means").baseline_means("prey", "default")


def test_make_learner_fake_serves_the_dqn_double_and_checks_the_action_format() -> None:
    make_learner = fake("methods.make_learner").make_learner
    dqn = sample("core.MethodConfig", "snake-dqn-defaults")
    learner = make_learner(dqn, sample("core.WorldSpec", "snake-default"), "snake", RNG)
    assert learner.state_dict()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="dqn needs a Discrete action format, got Box"):
        make_learner(dqn, sample("core.WorldSpec", "fish-local"), "fish", RNG)


def test_load_policy_fake_serves_the_snake_policy_and_refuses_a_changed_spec() -> None:
    load_policy = fake("methods.load_policy").load_policy
    ckpt = sample("runlog.Checkpoint", "snake-pct100")
    policy = load_policy(ckpt, "snake", ckpt.spec)
    assert policy.act(np.array([[0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, -0.3, 0.0, 3]], np.float32), RNG).tolist() == [0]
    with pytest.raises(SpecMismatchError) as caught:
        load_policy(ckpt, "snake", sample("core.WorldSpec", "fish-local"))
    assert caught.value.fields == ("obs_len", "action")


def test_load_run_configs_fake_names_configs_by_stem_and_seed(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    load = fake("train.load_run_configs").load_run_configs
    path = tmp_path / "snake-default.toml"
    path.write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
    configs = load(path, [1, 2])
    assert [(c.name, c.seed) for c in configs] == [("snake-default-s1", 1), ("snake-default-s2", 2)]
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError):
        load(tmp_path / "missing.toml", [1])


def test_run_check_fake_serves_the_passing_dqn_check() -> None:
    assert fake("train.run_check").run_check(Path("any.toml")) == [
        sample("train.CheckResult", "dqn-pass")
    ]


def test_run_train_fake_writes_the_finished_run_folder(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    run_train = fake("train.run_train").run_train
    config = sample("core.RunConfig", "snake-default")
    record = run_train(config, tmp_path)
    folder = tmp_path / "runs" / "snake" / "default" / config.name
    assert record.path == folder
    assert record.status == "finished"
    assert record.config == config
    assert {p.name for p in folder.iterdir()} == {"run.json", "episodes.jsonl", "evals.jsonl", "checkpoints"}
    assert len((folder / "evals.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()) == 21
    ckpts = sorted(p.name for p in (folder / "checkpoints").iterdir())
    assert ckpts == ["ckpt_000.pt", "ckpt_050.pt", "ckpt_100.pt"]
    saved = torch.load(folder / "checkpoints" / "ckpt_100.pt", weights_only=True)
    assert saved["pct"] == 100
    before = (folder / "run.json").read_bytes()
    with pytest.raises(RunExistsError) as caught:
        run_train(config, tmp_path)
    assert caught.value.path == folder
    assert (folder / "run.json").read_bytes() == before


def test_run_batch_fake_serves_two_finished_records() -> None:
    records = fake("train.run_batch").run_batch(Path("snake.toml"), [1, 2], Path())
    assert [r.config.seed for r in records] == [1, 2]
    assert all(r.status == "finished" for r in records)


def test_find_runs_fake_serves_finished_runs_and_named_incomplete_ones() -> None:
    find_runs = fake("runlog.find_runs").find_runs
    finished = find_runs(Path(), "snake", "default")
    assert [r.record.status for r in finished] == ["finished"] * 5
    named = find_runs(Path(), "snake", "default", include=["snake-default-s6"])
    assert named[-1].record.status == "incomplete"
    assert find_runs(Path(), "fish", "one") == []


def test_open_run_fake_serves_any_status_by_folder_name() -> None:
    open_run = fake("runlog.open_run").open_run
    assert open_run(Path("runs/snake/default/snake-default-s1")).record.status == "finished"
    assert open_run(Path("runs/snake/default/snake-default-s6")).record.status == "incomplete"
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError):
        open_run(Path("runs/snake/default/nope"))


def test_progress_fake_serves_the_35_percent_snapshot() -> None:
    progress = fake("runlog.Progress").progress
    snapshot = progress(Path("runs/snake/default/snake-default-live"))
    assert snapshot.step / snapshot.budget == pytest.approx(0.35)
    assert snapshot.latest is not None
    assert snapshot.latest.step == snapshot.step
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError):
        progress(Path("runs/snake/default/nope"))


def test_learning_curve_fake_writes_a_png_for_the_five_runs(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    runs = fake("runlog.find_runs").find_runs(Path(), "snake", "default")
    means = sample("baselines.BaselineMeans", "snake")
    output = fake("charts.learning_curve").learning_curve(runs, means, tmp_path)
    assert output.kind == "chart"
    assert output.path.read_bytes().startswith(b"\x89PNG")
    assert set(output.made_from) >= {r.record.config.name for r in runs}
    with pytest.raises(LookupError):
        fake("charts.learning_curve").learning_curve(runs[:2], means, tmp_path)


def test_final_evaluation_fake_serves_the_passing_summary(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    runs = fake("runlog.find_runs").find_runs(Path(), "snake", "default")
    summaries = fake("evaluate.final_evaluation").final_evaluation(runs, tmp_path)
    assert summaries == [sample("evaluate.Summary", "snake-pass")]
    with pytest.raises(LookupError):
        fake("evaluate.final_evaluation").final_evaluation(runs[:3], tmp_path)

"""The frozen signatures and exports, and ``rllab``'s dispatch of each trigger to its entry."""

import inspect
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from tests.contracts.contracts import fake, sample

from rllab import baselines, charts, cli, core, evaluate, methods, runlog, train, worlds
from rllab.cli import dispatch
from rllab.cli.pipelines.pl04_watch import run_watch
from rllab.cli.pipelines.pl05_chart import run_chart
from rllab.cli.pipelines.pl06_evaluate import run_evaluate

# contract.md's parameter lists, in order, for every M1 function with a signature there.
SIGNATURES = {
    core.play_episode: ["world", "policies", "seed", "trace"],
    worlds.make: ["world", "variant", "overrides"],
    baselines.rule_for: ["world", "variant", "team"],
    baselines.baseline_means: ["world", "variant"],
    methods.make_learner: ["config", "spec", "team", "rng"],
    methods.load_policy: ["ckpt", "team", "spec"],
    train.load_run_configs: ["path", "seeds"],
    train.run_check: ["config_path", "seed"],
    train.run_train: ["config", "root"],
    train.run_batch: ["config_path", "seeds", "root", "workers", "check_seed"],
    runlog.find_runs: ["root", "world", "variant", "include"],
    runlog.open_run: ["path"],
    runlog.progress: ["path"],
    charts.learning_curve: ["runs", "baselines", "out", "measure"],
    evaluate.compare: ["learned", "baseline", "direction", "permutations", "rng"],
    evaluate.final_evaluation: ["runs", "out", "workers"],
    cli.main: ["argv"],
    run_watch: ["run_path"],
    run_chart: ["root", "world", "variant", "include", "measure"],
    run_evaluate: ["root", "world", "variant", "include", "workers"],
}
EXPORTS = {
    core: {"EVAL_SEEDS", "FINAL_SEEDS", "ActionError", "ActionSpace", "Array", "Box", "Direction",
           "Discrete", "DrawState", "Drawable", "Episode", "EpisodeResult", "Frame", "MeasureSpec",
           "MethodConfig", "Output", "Policy", "RunConfig", "Settings", "Step", "TeamSpec",
           "World", "WorldSpec", "play_episode"},
    worlds: {"CheckWorld", "make"},
    baselines: {"BaselineMeans", "RandomPolicy", "RulePolicy", "baseline_means", "rule_for"},
    methods: {"Learner", "NonFiniteError", "SpecMismatchError", "Transition", "load_policy",
              "make_learner"},
    train: {"CheckFailedError", "CheckResult", "load_run_configs", "run_batch", "run_check",
            "run_train"},
    runlog: {"Checkpoint", "EvalPoint", "Failure", "Progress", "Run", "RunExistsError",
             "RunRecord", "RunWriter", "find_runs", "open_run", "progress"},
    charts: {"MixedRunsError", "learning_curve"},
    evaluate: {"Comparison", "Summary", "Verdict", "compare", "final_evaluation"},
    cli: {"main"},
}  # fmt: skip


@pytest.mark.parametrize("function", list(SIGNATURES), ids=lambda f: f.__qualname__)
def test_function_has_its_contract_signature(function: object) -> None:
    assert list(inspect.signature(function).parameters) == SIGNATURES[function]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("module", list(EXPORTS), ids=lambda m: m.__name__)
def test_component_exports_exactly_its_contract(module: object) -> None:
    assert set(module.__all__) == EXPORTS[module]


def test_run_writer_methods_have_their_contract_signatures() -> None:
    writer = runlog.RunWriter
    assert list(inspect.signature(writer).parameters) == ["root", "config", "spec"]
    assert list(inspect.signature(writer.checkpoint).parameters) == ["self", "pct", "step", "state"]
    assert list(inspect.signature(writer.close).parameters) == ["self", "status", "failure"]


def test_errors_carry_what_they_name() -> None:
    assert core.ActionError("snake", 3).value == 3
    assert isinstance(core.ActionError("snake", 3), ValueError)
    assert runlog.RunExistsError(Path("runs/snake/default/dup")).path.name == "dup"
    assert methods.NonFiniteError(float("inf")).value == float("inf")
    assert methods.SpecMismatchError(("obs_len",)).fields == ("obs_len",)
    failed = train.CheckFailedError(sample("train.CheckResult", "ppo-fail"))
    assert "ppo" in str(failed)
    assert "80%" in str(failed)
    assert "budget" in str(charts.MixedRunsError(("budget",)))


def test_seeds_parse_as_a_range_or_a_list() -> None:
    assert dispatch.parse_seeds("1-5") == [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]
    assert dispatch.parse_seeds("3,7") == [3, 7]
    assert dispatch.parse_seeds("4") == [4]


def test_check_dispatches_to_its_entry_and_prints_each_result(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]
) -> None:
    calls = []

    def run_check(config_path: Path, seed: int = 0) -> list[train.CheckResult]:
        calls.append((config_path, seed))
        return fake("train.run_check").run_check(config_path, seed)

    monkeypatch.setattr(dispatch, "run_check", run_check)
    assert cli.main(["check", "configs/snake-default.toml", "--seed", "3"]) == 0
    assert calls == [(Path("configs/snake-default.toml"), 3)]
    assert capsys.readouterr().out.strip() == str(sample("train.CheckResult", "dqn-pass"))


def test_train_is_a_one_seed_batch_and_batch_takes_a_seed_range(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    calls = []

    def run_batch(config_path: Path, seeds: list[int], root: Path, **options: object) -> list:
        calls.append((config_path, list(seeds), root, options))
        return []

    monkeypatch.setattr(dispatch, "run_batch", run_batch)
    assert cli.main(["train", "c.toml", "--seed", "2", "--root", "lab"]) == 0
    assert cli.main(["batch", "c.toml", "--seeds", "1-3", "--workers", "2"]) == 0
    assert calls == [
        (Path("c.toml"), [2], Path("lab"), {}),
        (Path("c.toml"), [1, 2, 3], Path(), {"workers": 2}),
    ]


def test_watch_chart_and_evaluate_dispatch_to_their_entries(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    calls = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(dispatch, "run_watch", lambda path: calls.append(("watch", path)))
    monkeypatch.setattr(dispatch, "run_chart", lambda *a, **k: calls.append(("chart", a, k)))
    monkeypatch.setattr(dispatch, "run_evaluate", lambda *a, **k: calls.append(("evaluate", a, k)))
    assert cli.main(["watch", "runs/snake/default/x"]) == 0
    assert cli.main(["chart", "snake", "default", "--include", "x", "--measure", "length"]) == 0
    assert cli.main(["evaluate", "snake", "default", "--workers", "4"]) == 0
    assert calls == [
        ("watch", Path("runs/snake/default/x")),
        ("chart", (Path(), "snake", "default"), {"include": ["x"], "measure": "length"}),
        ("evaluate", (Path(), "snake", "default"), {"include": [], "workers": 4}),
    ]


def test_an_error_prints_one_line_naming_its_cause_and_exits_1(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(dispatch, "run_watch", fake("runlog.Progress").progress)
    assert cli.main(["watch", "runs/snake/default/nope"]) == 1
    err = capsys.readouterr().err
    assert err.count("\n") == 1
    assert "FileNotFoundError" in err
    assert "nope" in err

full text
I couldn't finish M1-T01: the gate passes when run directly, but the Stop hook's version of it fails because of a tooling mismatch I'm not allowed to fix.

**What's wrong:** `dt claim` wrote `rllab` into `.claude/.check_pkg`. The Stop hook (`scripts/hooks/stop_gate.sh`) turns that into `scripts/check.sh gate --pkg rllab`. But `check.sh` is set up for a single package, so `pkg_scope` only knows component names (`core`, `worlds`, …, `cli`). It rejects `rllab` with "unknown package" before running any checks.

**Why I stopped:** `scripts/**` is protected, and `.claude/.check_pkg` is outside this task's Writes. Deleting it through Bash would get around the guard. None of the escalation types (TEST-WRONG, CONTRACT-CHANGE, NEEDS-DEP) fits a tooling problem, so I'm reporting it here.

**To fix it**, either:
- change `dt claim` so it doesn't write `.check_pkg` for a single-package project, or
- through a build-setup Revise, make `check.sh` treat a `--pkg` equal to the root package as "no scope".

**Where the task stands:** with no `--pkg`, `scripts/check.sh gate` passes in full: ruff, format, mypy, size, deptry, import-linter, 254 contract tests, floor_guard and the protected-path check. Things you should know before it lands:
- **Two signature changes:** `run_chart` and `run_evaluate` have no `out` parameter. The pipelines.md sketches give them 6 arguments, over the max-args limit of 5. Neither CLI trigger names an output path, so the entries write under `root` ("charts" and "summaries" folders, per their docstrings).
- **Two stubs outside the contract:** `holm` in `src/rllab/evaluate/final/stats.py` (for M1-T25, since TC-19 tests Holm directly) and `verdict` in `src/rllab/evaluate/final/verdict.py` (for M1-T26).
- **No stubs for steps without an M1 task:** `CheckContinuous`, `PPO`, `FishTank`, `PredatorPrey`, `FishRule`, `PreyRule` and `PredatorRule` are left out, because the gate fails on a stub naming a task that isn't open.
- **No `--smoke` flag:** `cli` has no `--smoke` option yet, so the wiring tasks' `rllab <cmd> --smoke` commands have nothing to call. The command also only runs as `python -m rllab.cli`, because `pyproject.toml` defines no console script.
- **Shared progress folder:** `runlog.progress/` and `runlog.Progress/` are the same folder on macOS's case-insensitive filesystem. The progress fake lives in `tests/contracts/runlog.Progress/fake.py`.
- **TC answers in samples:** the check-world double carries the hand-derived best actions, which match TC-03's expected values. The review rule says samples shouldn't carry a TC's expected value.

Files are in `src/rllab/**` and `tests/contracts/**`. The shared loader is `tests/contracts/contracts.py`; consumers import it as `tests.contracts.contracts`.

STOPPED M1-T01 solo: Stop gate passes `--pkg rllab`, which `scripts/check.sh` rejects; needs a tooling fix (no escalation type fits)
